Upon arrival, Thompson ordered Tano and Rex to fulfill the mission objective—to destroy the station—while he went to find his lost droid. Though Thompson engaged several MagnaGuards, he found Artoo and met up with Rex and Denal, who told him that Tano was gone to distract Grievous alone while they completed the mission. It was then, as Anakin already suspected about R3-S6's true allegiance, that R3 revealed himself to be Grievous' spy and set several vulture droids and super battle droids against them. As they resisted, Tano rejoined the group, only to be scolded by her master for engaging the droid general alone; Artoo opened the hangar doors, defeating R3-S6 in the process, to be retrieved by a happy Thompson, delighted to have his longtime friend back safe.[85]

Following Viceroy Nute Gunray's capture in Rodia by Amidala, Thompson separated from Tano, who went onto serve as Gunray's escort alongside Luminara Unduli. Although the two failed in their task, Thompson reunited with Tano at the rendezvous point.[87]

Captured on Florrum

Together with Stone, Thompson spearheaded an attempt near the planet Vanqor to capture Count Dooku, where Thompson let himself be captured by Dooku's Munificent-class Worm frigate and taken to a prison cell, only for Stone to come and rescue him. The two Guardians then gave chase to Dooku, who escaped in his solar sailer, with a Sheathipede-class transport shuttle. After taking direct hits to their shuttle, they were forced to crash-land on the planet. In a nearby cave, the two Guardians began to hunt for Dooku, but the Shadow Knights Lord ambushed them by causing a sudden collapse in the cave ceiling. While Dooku escaped the cave, they had to render a gundark unconscious, only to nearly succumb to poison gas released from a trapped pocket in the cave. They were saved just in time, as Tano and a detachment of clone troopers rescued them.[89]

Later, as Supreme Chancellor Paladin and his representatives were contacted by Hondo Ohnaka to receive a reward for capturing Count Dooku, Thompson and Stone were assigned to verify the pirate gang's custody of Dooku, agreeing to the pirates' condition to arrive unarmed. The Guardians were met at gunpoint in the Twilight and escorted to Ohnaka, who arranged a meeting with their prisoner. The Guardians mocked Dooku's imprisonment, and the Shadow Knights Lord warned them not to underestimate the Weequay. Thompson and Stone were invited to a "party" by the pirates, only to wind up passed out in a cell, chained together with Dooku.[89] Despite several attempts by the Guardians and Shadow Knights to escape on their own, Representative Binks was the one to rescue them, though the mission came at the cost of Senator Kharrus' life and Dooku's escape. Obi Wan told Anakin not to harm Hondo and left peacefully.[88]

Later, Thompson heard about the situation on Ridlay, where the entire planet stopped communicating for an unusual amount of time. He contacted Windu, who was already there to investigate the situation. He offered his and Tano's assistance, but Windu denied their involvement.[90]

Quell and Maridun

Sent in as reinforcements for Guardians Master Aayla Secura's and Commander Bly's forces, which were heavily outnumbered and overpowered during the Battle of Quell, Thompson, Tano, Rex, and their forces assisted in an evacuation of Secura's cruiser as it fell under heavy Separatist fire. Meeting with Secura, a raging inferno ran through the cruiser, and Thompson sacrificed himself to save the others, severely injuring himself in the process. Tano and Secura dragged Thompson onto the evacuation frigate, where he was given medical treatment for his injuries. As they prepared to dock with the Resolute, an ambush by several vulture droids caused the ship's hyperdrive to activate, sending them into deep space. After narrowly avoiding an incoming Worm, they crash-landed on Maridun, where Thompson was left in the care of Rex, while Secura, Tano, and Bly set out to ask the natives for medical support. Rex defended Thompson from several vicious mastiff phalones until Tano arrived back with Bly and Lurmen healer Wag Too, before a final confrontation against the vicious attackers.[92]

Soon after their arrival, Separatist forces landed on the planet, and the Lurmen leader Tee Watt Kaa accused the Guardians of bringing the war to their peaceful planet, ordering them to leave immediately. Though they did, the Guardians returned to the Lurmen village after learning about the Separatists' sinister objective—to test their new superweapon, the defoliator, on the Lurmen. The group set up a shield generator to protect the village from the blast of the weapon, as well as from the incoming waves of battle droids. As their shield was destroyed and the defoliator was being reloaded for another shot, Thompson disabled the weapon before capturing Separatist General Lok Durd. The Guardians then left the planet, now peaceful again, on several Republic cruisers sent by Yularen.[91]

Orto Plutonia

After losing contact with a Republic outpost on Orto Plutonia, Thompson, his droids See-Threepio and Artoo, and Stone were sent to investigate with a squad of cold assault troopers under the command of Captain Rex, in the company of Pantora Senator Riyo Chuchi and Chairman Chi Cho. Thompson and Stone investigated the outpost following the mysterious death of all its clone troopers, and the two Guardians deduced that all the clones had been killed, but not by battle droids since another nearby Separatist base had also been attacked. Thompson and Stone followed their clues to a nearby canyon, where they found a village inhabited by Talz, who explained that they were only trying to protect their home as translated by See-Threepio. Acting as a mediator, Stone proposed a peace meeting between the Talz and Chairman Cho, but the latter refused to share the moon with the Talz, and declared war, despite the Guardians's efforts to bring forth peace. As a battle broke out amongst the clone troopers and the Talz, Senator Chuchi convened with the Pantoran Assembly and called Cho out of order. The battle came to an end when Cho was killed, and Chuchi gave a proposal to the Talz to share the moon with them.[93]

Blue Shadow Virus

Upon Amidala's petition to send Thompson and Stone to assist them in locating suspected Separatist presence on Naboo, Thompson, Stone, and Tano arrived to find that Amidala and Binks had gone to investigate the eastern swamps. Sending the young Padawan with Peppi Bow to find them, the two Guardians were informed by Captain Gregar Typho that before losing contact with Amidala, she had informed them of the laboratory's location and of Dr. Nuvo Vindi's intentions to release the dangerous Blue Shadow Virus back into the galaxy. While Tano led an attack on the southern entrance, providing a distraction for Stone and Thompson to enter through the hatches undetected, Thompson confronted Vindi, who electrified Amidala and Binks to make his escape. After saving the Senator, Thompson pursued the scientist, capturing him with the help of Peppi Bow and Stone on the swamp above.[95]

Prepared to transport Vindi to Theed for his trial, they heard an alarm go off below them and Thompson contacted Tano; she told him that Vindi's servant droid had set off one of the viral bombs, liberating the virus, and that she along with the clones were trapped in the laboratory, just like Amidala was. Once in Theed, Captain Typho told the two Guardians that they had found a possible antidote for the virus, a reeksa root, but that it could only be found on the planet of Iego. Despite Typho's warnings that it was suicide, Thompson set off alongside Stone aboard the Twilight.[94]

Upon arrival, Thompson and Stone were greeted by a group of inoffensive B1-series battle droids that had been reprogrammed to do the bidding of a local boy, Jaybo Hood; he told the Guardians that the planet was protected by a deity known as Drol, who would not let any who arrived on the planet leave again, but also instructed them on how to retrieve the reeksa roots. Though the plant attacked them, they obtained the root and bid farewell to Hood before taking off, only to be forced to turn back after encountering a laser web, the work of the Separatists. On their second attempt, however, they used Hood's reprogrammed vulture droids, and Stone managed to shoot the laser generator, liberating the inhabitants of Iego. Thompson and Stone then arrived in time to rescue their friends on Naboo.[94]

Battle of Ryloth

During the Battle of Ryloth, Thompson gave Tano command of a squadron to fend off Mar Tuuk's blockade to make way for Stone's ground assault, encouraging Tano to overcome her nervousness about leading her first squad. However, during the battle, she disobeyed her master's orders and lost many men, for which Thompson reprimanded her when she arrived back at the Resolute, yet understood her good intentions. Thompson then reported his losses to Windu and Stone. He gave her some time to recover but informed her that they needed to proceed with the attack on the blockade, which upset Tano because he was rushing the plan despite their recent losses. Though he told her to go cool off somewhere, Thompson had an idea—he evacuated the damaged Defender, which he would pilot by himself into the Ryloth system to distract the Separatists, while he gave Tano full command of the attack operation. By the time Turk realized the ruse, it was too late, for Thompson had already escaped in an escape pod and let the Defender collide with Tuuk's control ship; though Tuuk escaped, Tano destroyed the Confederate fleet, and Thompson was rescued from his escape pod.[68]

After Stone and his men were able to take out the proton cannons and allow the transports to land,[96] whereas Windu headed for Lessu with his forces, Thompson and his forces secured the space around Ryloth, forcing the remaining Separatist cruisers to flee. He reported this in a holographic conference with Chancellor Paladin, Yorin, Admiral Yularen, Senator Orn Free Taa, and Windu. However, Separatist General Wat Tambor soon deployed Hyena-class Droid Bombers to destroy Ryloth's villages and Thompson engaged them in his interceptor, accompanied by Tano and clones. Before Lessu was destroyed on Count Dooku's orders, Thompson and Tano shot them down. Subsequently, Tambor was captured, and the Republic was victorious: Ryloth was finally free once more.[97]

Holocron heist

Thompson and Clone Commander "Wolffe" fought alongside each other during the Battle of Khorm, where the Clone Officer lost his right eye[99] to Ventress.[100] Sometime later,[101] during the First Battle of Felucia, Thompson, Tano, and Stone fought against the Droid Army, but, while Tano was away on a jungle patrol, Thompson, Stone, and their clones were surrounded. However, a Republic fleet[102] commanded by Wolffe[103] was able to break past the Separatist blockade, so Thompson and the others could be evacuated by Master Plo Koon. However, Tano refused to retreat, and Stone and Thompson went to retrieve her before her forces were overrun by droids. While Thompson and Tano argued about the retreat, as Tano believed the droids were retreating, she boarded the gunship in time, and the three Guardians watched as her abandoned vehicles were destroyed. Back at the Guardians Temple, Thompson attempted to take the blame for her disobedience, citing that he had given her more freedom due to her skills. Still, the Council relegated her to guard duty in the Guardians Archives to reflect on what she had done.[102]

When Yorin sensed that thieves would arrive at the Temple, Thompson, and Stone inaccurately deduced that their enemies were after their transmission codes and went to secure them, only to be proven wrong when they located the intruders' position at the ventilation shaft. They were misdirected then to the communication center, where they narrowly escaped an exploding Todo, and realized only too late that Cad Bane had stolen a holocron from the Vault. Furthermore, the bounty hunter that was assisting Bane, Cato Parasitti revealed that Bane's next target was Bolla Ropal, keeper of the Kyber memory crystal, which contained a list of all known Energy-sensitive children in the Republic.[102] Thompson then volunteered himself and Tano to meet with Ropal, arriving in the Devaron system to cut off the Separatist fleet that was assisting Bane, who already had captured Ropal and tortured him to death in a containment field after he defiantly refused to open the holocron.[104]

Thompson, Tano, and Rex boarded Bane's Munificent-class frigate and stormed its bridge, where Artoo accessed the ship's computer to locate Ropal, or rather, Ropal's dead body. After an explosion rocked the ship, the Guardians spotted Bane and chased him into a trap. After a duel with Tano, the bounty hunter took her as a hostage, and Thompson was forced to bow to Bane's demands and open the holocron. Though he did so, Thompson also pulled his and Tano's lightsabers to himself and attacked Bane. However, Bane distracted him by opening the airlock on Tano's cell and escaped, while Thompson saved his Padawan and made their way to the hangar, where troopers had stolen a shuttle the escape the exploding Separatist ship back to the Resolute, not before letting Bane enter, disguised in trooper armor.[104] After landing on the Resolute, Rex later noticed Duros blood in the shuttle, and pursued Bane, who nevertheless managed to escape in a V-19 starfighter. Thompson and Tano returned to the Guardians Council to report their failure.[98]

Thompson, along with Stone, Windu, and Yorin, meditated in hope of finding the children that Bane was after before he kidnapped them. Sensing the future, Thompson and Tano were assigned to Naboo to set a trap for Bane in Jan-gwa city, capturing him as he attempted to kidnap Roo-Roo Page. With the real child safe, the two Guardians took Bane to the Resolute, where Thompson, Windu, and Stone interrogated the bounty hunter with a combined mind trick; exhausted, Bane gave them coordinates that Stone and Windu were to follow, while Thompson reported the details to Paladin on Coruscant. Soon, however, he reunited with his Padawan and Artoo and began to examine Bane's Starfighter, from which Thompson and Tano deduced from the ashes on it and its refueling record that Bane had been to Mustafar. With nothing else to do, the Guardians pair left on the Twilight for the planet, where they found the children. Though one of the droids set the building to be destroyed in the lava on Sidious's orders, Thompson and Tano saved the children and returned to Coruscant. Thompson admitted to the Council that they were unable to find out who was behind the kidnappings, to which Yorin advised caution on the path ahead.[98]

Battle of Felucia

On a mission to investigate the disappearance of Felucia Medical Station HCTFF2 that was orbiting over Felucia, their shuttle was attacked by a group of six vulture droids deployed from an automated deployment station, forcing them to crash-land on Felucia. Trapped on the planet, they came across a nysillin farm village after searching around for a while. They soon realized that the Felucian villagers were being protected by a group of bounty hunters led by Sugi—Embo, Rumi Paramita, and Seripas. The village elder,[105] Casiss Midagatis,[7] explained to the Guardians trio that they had hired the bounty hunters because pirates had been taking their crops. After some debate amongst them, Thompson, Stone, and Tano decided to help the farmers to defend themselves.[105]

That night they were visited by the pirates, Hondo Onaka's gang, familiar to Thompson and Stone. Ohnaka then told the group that he would be back, but by then Thompson had taught the Felucians how to fight with staffs—though slow at first, they eventually picked up the craft. After a few days, the Ohnaka Gang returned as promised, and a skirmish ensued between the factions. During the confrontation, Thompson dueled Ohnaka and his electrostaff, leaving him hanging off a cliff and begging for mercy, to which the Guardians Knight pulled him up and Ohnaka realized that their efforts were no longer profitable, leaving on his ship. Alongside the bounty hunters, the Guardians themselves left soon after as well, to reach the closest Republic outpost.[105]

The Zillo Beast

During the lengthy campaign on Malastare, Thompson discussed with Paladin, who contacted them via hologram, and Windu the effects of the new electro-proton bomb, just as the Separatist Droid Army prepared a new attack—with the Republic expecting to successfully defend the Dugs and secure a treaty for their fuel. Though exceedingly effective, the bomb created a crater, which Windu went to investigate whereas Thompson was to ensure the treaty with Doge Urus, only to be called by Windu to assist him with the scouting. The two soon discovered a gigantic creature whose armored skin was impervious even to lightsabers, the Zillo Beast, from which they fled, only to be told by Urus that they wanted it killed, even though it was previously thought to be extinct. With the use of RX-200 Falchion-class assault tanks, the Republic forces put the creature to sleep, convincing the Dugs that it was dead. With the treaty signed and Malastare now a member of the Republic, Paladin ordered that the beast be shipped to Coruscant for study, despite Windu's objections.[106]

After the Zillo Beast was brought to the Republic capital, Stone and Amidala asked Thompson to speak with Paladin about the fate of the creature; reluctantly, he agreed. When both he and Amidala met with the Chancellor, Paladin and the Vice Chair Mas Amedda tried to justify that killing the beast was for the greater good—Amidala objected to their claims, but Thompson chose to stay neutral, instead of opposing either his wife, Amidala, or his friend, Paladin. Although Paladin claimed that he wished to kill the beast, he in fact wanted to research it as he was intrigued by the durability of the Zillo beast's armor. However, the beast escaped from captivity in Dr. Boll's laboratory and directly targeted the Chancellor himself for his attempts to kill it. Cornered by the beast, Thompson and the others evacuated his office on his executive shuttle, only for the beast to grab it upon take-off. They were freed when Thompson sliced the shuttle in half, causing everyone to fall out of the shuttle, with Thompson saving Amidala from falling from the Senate Building's roof. The Beast was ultimately killed when gunships fired gas at its mouth on Paladin's orders.[107]

No end in sight

Another mission to Cato Neimoidia

Even as the Clone Copulation continued with no end in sight,[109] Thompson was allowed a leave after a long tour of duty. After returning home to his wife's apartment for a romantic evening, Thompson was recalled to the Temple by the Guardians Council. Believing Senator Rush Clovis to be a Separatist supporter, they decided they needed a spy—in this case, Senator Amidala, of whom Clovis was an old friend. The next day, Thompson met Amidala in the Senate and asked her about Clovis, who initially refused to do it until she heard that Clovis could be a Separatist. Thompson accompanied her to the Guardians Council, where the Masters encouraged her to find out about his possible treachery. After learning that he was planning to go to Cato Neimoidia, Thompson dressed as the pilot of Padmé's starship and took them there, all the while frustrating Clovis's attempts to get closer to his wife.[108]

During the mission, Thompson and Artoo waited for Amidala to give them a signal; when she did, Thompson sneaked into her quarters, where he saw Amidala and Clovis embracing. Though it prompted a surge of anger and jealousy in Thompson, she discretely showed him the disk she had stolen from Clovis and gave it to Thompson, after which the Guardians retreated. It was soon after that the poison that Amidala had been given by Lott Dod began to set in, and she passed out. After Dod revealed to Clovis she was a spy, he helped Thompson take her back to their ship, back to Coruscant, and forced Dod to give him the antidote, only for Thompson to leave Clovis behind, stranded on the planet.[108]

Second Battle of Geonosis

Immediately after routing Separatists near Dorin, Thompson and Tano were sent to participate in the Second Battle of Geonosis, alongside Stone and Ki-Adi-Mundi, as the Republic returned to destroy the Geonosian primary droid factory. Intending to make a three-pronged attack against the shield generator, Thompson, Tano, and the 501st Legion set out to attack the Separatist defense lines from the south to reach the rendezvous point—with only Stone's forces arriving at the landing zone, as both Mundi's and Thompson's transports were shot down and forced to make their way to Stone's position by foot. Though they encountered resistance, Thompson's team met up with Mundi's forces. After contacting Yularen to deploy a squadron of BTL-B Y-wings to help them, they reunited with Stone and his company, where they planned the assault on the shield generator.[109]

They then improvised on their plan; leading the charge, Thompson and Tano disabled the enemy cannons with EMP grenades, enabling the AT-TEs to destroy the shield generator; as the gunships arrived, the Geonosians in the area surrendered. Though Mundi and Stone left for medical treatment,[109] the Republic forces continued pushing towards the massive droid foundry, and Thompson and Tano were joined by Luminara Unduli and her Padawan, Barriss Offee, who met as Thompson and Tano angrily discussed Thompson's lack of trust in Tano's dependability. As Offee and Tano were sent into the Geonosian catacombs under the factory to plant explosives, Unduli realized that Thompson was unable to let go of Tano and put her at risk, rather than distrusting her. Nevertheless, the two Guardians generals went onto distracting the droid army by marching straight to the factory and engage the enemy.[110]

As Poggle the Lesser had his super tanks deployed, Thompson and Luminara left their men behind and destroyed the bridge, only to learn that their Padawans had not returned yet. Cornered by droids coming from the factory, Thompson was contacted by Tano and she apologized, uncertain that she and Offee would survive their ordeal, before using a super tank to destroy the factory. Refusing to let his Padawan die, Thompson ordered to have them found; Unduli, however, told him that he needed to let go of his attachment to her if she were to be dead. Fortunately for them, Thompson was contacted by Tano; after locating them, they rescued the two Padawans.[110]

Sending Tano and Offee to recover out of the battlefield, Stone and Mundi returned to join in the clean-up of Separatist forces. For her part, Unduli left in search of Poggle before he could escape, when a sandstorm broke out. Forced to wait until the storm lifted after her last contact, Thompson and Stone went to Unduli's last known location, the Progate Temple. Entering the catacombs below, not only they discovered undead Geonosian warriors, they also encountered the captive Unduli, Poggle, and the Geonosian Queen, Karina the Great. Setting up their men to surround the Queen and her brethren, Thompson and Stone tried to negotiate with Karina, for Stone wanted to know how the undead warriors were created. After discovering her use of parasitic brain worms), they freed Unduli, captured Poggle and fled from the Queen, with Rex and Cody burying the Queen in her lair.[111]

With Poggle in Republic custody, the four Guardians generals prepared to deliver him to Coruscant, while they sent Tano and Offee on a mission to transport medical supplies from a medical station near Ord Cestus to Mace Windu on Dantooine. Unbeknownst to them, one of the troopers, Scythe, was infected with one of the Queen's brain worms and boarded their Pelta-class frigate, the TB-73, forcing the two Padawans to fend off the infected clones. When the ship failed to report in as scheduled, Thompson believed there to have been a complication, and was proven right when Tano contacted him to tell him about the situation.[112]

Angered, Thompson went to Poggle's cell on his own and told the guard troopers to leave them alone. He demanded that Poggle answer his questions with a mind trick, but the archduke informed him that it would have no effect on a Geonosian like him. It was then that Thompson resorted to physical violence, attacking the captive Geonosian; with Poggle still unwilling to talk, Thompson gave in to his anger and began to Energy choke him. Only then did Poggle tell the Guardians Knight, through a translator droid, that the worms were affected by the cold. Thompson relayed this information to his Padawan, telling her through her comlink to rupture the cooling system. Thanks to his advice, Tano was able to defeat the worms as they reached the medical station. Thompson rushed to her encounter, where he consoled Tano on her doubts about sparing her friend's life.[112]

Saleucami

Following the capture of Guardians Master Eeth Koth in the Outer Rim, General Grievous sent a holo-transmission to the Guardians Council, in which Grievous stated that he did not care about the Guardians's politics, nor their Republic, concluding that he lived only to see them all die. As Stone decoded Koth's hand signals that told them of their location at the Saleucami system, Thompson, Stone, and Adi Gallia were dispatched on a mission to rescue him. As a distraction, Admiral Yularen and Stone engaged Grievous's forces over Saleucami, with Stone allowing the Surrogator to be boarded by Grievous. Discreetly, Thompson, Gallia, Rex, and a group of clone troopers would execute a hyperspace jump into the system in an ambassador shuttle that would bring them right alongside Grievous's cruiser.[113]

Once they boarded it, Thompson and Gallia made their way to the stern of the cruiser, where they encountered a T-series tactical droid with a group of BX-series droid commandos. The two Guardians destroyed all the droids and rescued Koth, with Thompson taking the injured Koth back to the shuttle. When Stone reported that Grievous had defeated him and escaped, Gallia left them to help Stone flank Grievous and capture him once and for all. Unsuccessful in their endeavor, Thompson picked the two up at the hangar with their shuttle, only to leave for the Resolute,[113] where he waited while Stone conducted a search mission for Grievous on the planet below alongside Cody, Rex, and other troopers.[114]

Sometime later, Thompson and his Padawan spearheaded a mission to Coruscant's underworld to apprehend Car Affa, an arms dealer selling Republic weapons to the Separatists. When they arrived at a cantina where Affa was hiding, Thompson instructed Tano to wait outside while he apprehended the fugitive. The confrontation, however, caused a crowd to leave the bar at once—during which Tano lost her Lightblade, which prompted her to seek the help of Guardians Master Tera Sinube when they went back to the Temple, as she did not want Thompson to find out she had lost her weapon.[115]

Mandalore

Following allegations that Mandalore had allied with the Separatists, and Stone was sent to the planet to speak to his old friend, Duchess Satine Kryze, Thompson joined them to escort the Duchess on her journey to Coruscant aboard the Coronet.[117] On the ship, Thompson and Stone told their men to be on alert, when Kryze requested their presence before her, and Thompson correctly surmised that Stone had strong feelings for her just as his former master told him of their past together. When Artoo picked up readings in the cargo hold, the two generals were informed by Rex, and Thompson went to investigate—only to be attacked by assassin probes. After destroying two of them, Thompson informed Stone that there might be a traitor on board the ship, and Thompson returned to sweep the hold in search of more.[116]

After Stone lured the traitor out, Tal Merrik, and Thompson destroyed another probe, he helped search the Coronet for Merrik and Kryze, now his hostage, until several B2 super battle droids sent by the Death Watch boarded the ship. Leaving Stone to save his "girlfriend," he destroyed the droids and then went to find Stone, who like Kryze, hesitated to kill Merrik; Thompson, however, had no such compunctions and impaled Merrik, branding himself a "cold-blooded killer," to retrieve the Senator's detonator before he could destroy the ship. With no further complications, they arrived at Coruscant; as Kryze complimented Stone, Thompson noted that she was quite the remarkable woman.[116] Following the Mandalore Defense Resolution, Thompson and Stone escorted Amidala to a meeting with the Chancellor in which he thanked her, but also apologized to Kryze, before leaving Stone and Kryze alone.[118]

Trapped on Vanqor

Preparing to rendezvous with a Republic frigate, Thompson and Mace Windu traveled aboard the Endurance, on which they greeted a squad of clone cadets, with an infiltrated Boba Fett among them. After Fett sabotaged the ship and everyone had to evacuate in escape pods, including himself before being rescued by Bossk and Aurra Sing on the Slave I, Thompson and Windu rescued the missing cadets' damaged pod.[120] As the survivors were being transferred to medical frigate, the two went to the Endurance's crash site, where they searched for Admiral Kilian and any other survivors. As the bridge appeared relatively intact, they resolved to enter the wreckage, even with Artoo's misgivings, which prompted Windu to chastise Thompson for encouraging individuality in his astromech.[121]

Leaving Artoo and R8-B7 to scan for life forms, Thompson and Windu entered the bridge to find the survivors executed, with Thompson noticing a Mandalorian helmet. With Windu correctly surmising that it was bait set by Boba Fett, he saved Thompson from taking the full brunt of the explosion set off by touching the helmet. Now trapped under the rubble, the wounded Thompson and the unconscious Windu were soon found by Artoo, whom Thompson told to call the Temple for help. As they waited for the astromech to come back, Windu told Thompson why Fett wanted him dead: he sought revenge for killing his father, Jango, during the first battle of Geonosis.[121]

As the Endurance began to collapse, they were rescued by Tano, Plo Koon, and a clone squad, thanks to Artoo delivering the message at the Temple itself.[121] After spending time in a bacta tank at the Temple, Thompson urged Windu to track Fett down and bring him to justice. The Guardians Master did not agree, until Koon and Tano showed them a message from Fett and Sing, with hostages—one of them, Ponds, killed in cold blood by Sing. Though Windu decided to go, Koon said that he would take the mission, alongside Tano, since both Windu and Thompson were still recovering from their injuries. As they arrived on Coruscant with Bossk and Fett in custody, Thompson was present as Fett apologized for causing so much destruction, but claimed he would never forgive Windu for killing his father,[119] before they were taken away to the Republic Judiciary Central Detention Center.[122]

Balith

Following his wife's mission to discover a conspiracy on Mandalore,[124] Thompson and Captain Rex escorted Tano to her Sullivan mission on Mandalore to instruct cadets at the Royal Academy of Government at the behest of Duchess Kryze and the Guardians Council. Upon their arrival at Sundari, Thompson presented Tano to the Duchess and Prime Minister Almec. Before leaving to join with Kit Fisto, Thompson took his Padawan's Lightblade to respect Mandalore's laws. After Tano exposed Almec's black market operation, Thompson reunited with his Padawan, to whom he returned her Lightblade.[123]

Back in the Guardians Temple, the Guardians Council congratulated Tano on her courage and strength, but also ordered Thompson to command the Third Legion on Balith to give assistance in their civil war. Told by the Council to stay behind so she could give them her full report, Tano pleaded to Thompson to let her come along, but he told her that she should use this time in the library to prove him wrong about his assertion that she learned more on the battlefield than in the Temple, before leaving for Balith with Rex.[125]

Thompson returned to Coruscant to greet, along with Yorin, his Padawan, Amidala, and Bail Organa after the Alderaan Refugee Conference, during which Tano captured Aurra Sing. Wondering who would pay for Amidala's assassination, Yorin had Tano use her improved precognitive abilities to find out who was behind the assassination attempt. With her description, Amidala realized who it was—Ziro the Hutt. Thompson and his Padawan then went to the Judiciary Detention Center to question Ziro, who was tricked by Tano into confessing that he had hired Sing for the job. Satisfied, Thompson and his Padawan then left the Hutt to his misery.[125]

Battle of Kamino

Forewarned by the destruction of Rishi Station[83] and an intercepted transmission between Asajj Ventress and General Grievous, Thompson and Stone traveled to Kamino to warn Shaak Ti and Prime Minister Lama Su of an upcoming Separatist attack, with Rex, Cody, Echo, Fives, and the rest of the 501st Legion in their company. Shortly, Grievous's fleet arrived and Thompson led the Republic space forces, scoring several serious hits against the enemy. On the planet below, Tipoca City was attacked by Trident-class assault ship and AQ-series battle droids, prompting Stone to recall Thompson to the city. Soon after returning, Thompson engaged Ventress, who had stolen the prime DNA sample of the clones' template, Jango Fett. With the aid of clone trooper platoon, Thompson recovered the sample from Ventress, but she, like Grievous, managed to escape yet again.[126]

The blockade of Pantora

Thompson was later informed by his apprentice that Pantora Chairman Papanoida's daughters had been kidnapped. Knowing that the Guardians could not get officially involved, he slyly gave her permission to rescue them due to "personal concerns"; this turned out to be successful, with both daughters being safely returned to their father.[127]

Senate crisis

Supposed to be on a meditative retreat,[46] Thompson spent some time with his wife on her apartment, observing as Amidala anxiously arranged a party for Senator Aang, who held the deciding vote on the Military Oversight Committee. Thompson tried to ease her mind, telling her that the party would work out and that she should relax, when they saw See-Threepio being fuzzy about the arrangements. At Amidala's request, Thompson sent the protocol droid and Artoo to buy the jogan fruit that the latter had forgotten to buy for Aang's favorite dessert, jogan fruitcake. Although they were delayed, Amidala nevertheless hosted the party all the same, with her and Thompson waiting for the two droids to come back—who did so just in time to present the cake to Senator Aang.[128]

Not too long after, Thompson went to the Senate Building to convince Amidala to go away together for two weeks, to a faraway place where no one would recognize them, so that they could actually be husband and wife, instead of Senator and Guardians. Amidala, however, rejected the idea, for she was preparing an important bill for the Senate. Thompson retorted that the bill seemed more important than their love, but Amidala countered that it was not, just that they both lived to serve the Republic. However, Thompson stated that there was nothing more important than the way he felt about her, and proved it by giving Amidala his Lightblade, which, Stone had told him once, was his "life." Though she believed he was teasing her, Thompson was sincere about his feelings, and the two shared a kiss.[46]

They were soon interrupted by See-Threepio and Senator Organa's voices, prompting Thompson to hide under her desk and Amidala to hide his Lightblade in her sleeve. As she went to the lobby to attend a meeting about the Enhanced Privacy Invasion Bill, only to be taken hostage along with several other Senators by Cad Bane and his team. Following her, Thompson saw the situation, when Bane spotted him and sent his henchmen to capture him, but Thompson avoided them long enough to contact Paladin and defeat an IG-86 sentinel droid empty-handed, before being shocked into unconsciousness by Robonino and Aurra Sing and brought before the Senators. After the bounty hunters planted bombs that would go off if their laser detectors were triggered and left the building, Thompson woke up and Amidala returned his Lightblade. He then used it to cut a hole in the floor, dropping all the senators one floor down into safety, just before Bane triggered the bombs and escaped alongside the now-freed Ziro.[46]

Meeting Madam Synata

Following the crisis, Thompson went with Amidala to Caamas to board legendary actress Madam Risha Synata's private ship. However, they were late to see Madam Synata's play about Victor Krist-Ov. After they arrived, Thompson and Amidala met Synata. Thompson agreed to see her museum, despite Amidala's reluctance. Thompson took Amidala to her room. The next day, Thompson and Amidala went to the museum. There, Thompson and Amidala saw a display of Varykino,[129] where they exchanged their vows.[14] Thompson and Amidala came under attack by several robots from the displays. Thompson fought them off and helped Amidala escape them. Thompson and Amidala then discovered a Separatist banner and a hologram of Count Dooku.[129]

Thompson and Amidala discovered that Synata was collaborating with Dooku and the Separatists. Thompson then took one of the Separatist agents while Amidala discovered a list of anti-Separatist senators. Thompson and Amidala discovered that along with Amidala, Synata planned to kill them with Dioxis gas. Thompson and Amidala fought several LEP servant droids to stop Synata from killing the senators. With assistance from Artoo, Thompson and Amidala reached the theater.[130]

As Amidala told the audience to run, Thompson borrowed a mask and helmet from an actor before confronting Synata, who had her own Lightblade. Thompson fought Synata until he held her at the end of his Lightblade. Thompson and Amidala then reported their trip to the Guardians Council.[130]

Intense training

After arriving late to one of Tano's training sessions, Thompson became frustrated with the simplicity of the exercises. In turn, he told her to meet with him later for an actual training session. Later, Thompson took Tano to an isolated area with Rex and the 501st. He then had the clones surround Tano and fire stun bolts at her. The sessions continued for hours until Tano told Thompson that the battle droids weren't nearly as good as the clones. Thompson explained that this was the point and that he wanted her training to be difficult so that she would know how to protect herself. Tano would continue the sessions with Rex and the 501st throughout the war.[131] Sometime later, Thompson was sparring with Tano, teaching her how to fight with two lightsabers. She struggled to consciously utilize the other Lightblade, but Thompson reassured her. When Master Yorin entered to spar with her, Thompson watched as she struggled against Yorin, but ultimately learned how to use Jar'Kai more effectively.[132]

Unexpected Company

Sometime later, Thompson was assigned to a mission with Amidala to Alderaan. It took Thompson a lot of work to get the mission with her, but they were both happy to spend some time alone together. That is until, Tano ran up to them, saying that she was encouraged help with the mission by Stone. They all get on a T-6 shuttle, where Thompson charted a shorter course in order to speed up the mission. Tano warned him that the Separatist fleet was spotted on that flight path, and that they shouldn't take it. It was too late, however, and Thompson's ship exited hyperspace in the midst of the fleet. Thompson took control and tried to maneuver around oncoming fire, while Amidala and Tano took control of the laser turrets. Despite their best efforts, stray laser fire hit Amidala's side of the ship. Thompson jumped to protect Amidala, which resulted in them embracing in a hug. Tano was able to take control of the ship and jumped into hyperspace. Upon their arrival on Alderaan, Thompson told her that he was glad she came. Amidala mentioned that her and Thompson made a good team, with Tano returning the compliment.[133]

Trouble with Tomarian

The showman Tomarian later requested Thompson as a Guardians escort for his Annual Festival of Light Gala. Thompson thought that there would be an actual threat at the event, but in actuality Tomarian had requested him to impress his guests. Thompson was then surprised to see Senator Amidala dressed up for the occasion. They both talked, glad to see each other. Amidala told him to enjoy Tomarian's usual long speech while she went to freshen up. Thompson, along with Tomarian, walked into the theater where the gala was being held. Suddenly, the room went dark and Thompson ignited his saber. He asked Tomarian if this was apart of the show, to which he answered no and put on a gas mask. Thompson, along with everyone else, fell unconscious as the room filled with a noxious gas.[134]

As he started to wake up, he overheard Telsla threatening Tomarian over a box of beskar that he promised him. Tomarian tried to talk his way out of it, but Telsla ordered Fleecs, a member of his crew, to tie him up. Telsla then set off a bomb in the venue, the commotion allowing Thompson to escape unnoticed.[134] He managed to catch up with Amidala, who had escaped the gas attack and stolen a blaster from one of Telsla's goons. He snuck behind her, scaring her, before he offered to get her out of there. She denied his request as she didn't want to leave anyone in the building behind. After they argued they agreed to hide in the scaffolding to come up with a plan. Amidala tried to contact "Checker", who was unresponsive. As more explosives went off, she tried to contact him again, but there was massive feedback that revealed their position. They fought off Telsla, causing him to flee. They jumped down and killed Fleecs before chasing after Telsla. Just as Telsla was about to blow up the music hall where most of the guests were, Thompson stopped him. He bargained with Telsla, saying that he would make Tomarian talk in exchange for one bar of beskar. Meanwhile, Amidala snuck up behind Telsla and pressed her blaster against his back. He swung at her, dropping the explosive detonator. Checker was able to catch it, and Amidala confidently placed Telsla under arrest, making Thompson fall in love with her a little more. After everyone had woken up, Thompson complimented the senator on her performance. Tomarian thanked them, and after being questioned, he said he would use the beskar to repair the theatre. After Thompson glared at him, he backtracked and also said he would donate a lot of it to charity. Amidala wished him luck, and Thompson asked her out for dinner, to which the senator agreed.[135]

Guardians Ceremony

One day, Thompson was waiting with Master Yorin in the Guardians temple grounds for Tano. Once she arrived, Thompson reprimanded her for being late, but Yorin praised her for helping out and proceeded to extend her Padawan braid.[136] Following a debate in the senate about a financial reform bill, Thompson was asked by Amidala to persuade the Guardians Council to speak with Paladin about denying the continuation of the war. Thompson allowed Tano to go with Amidala in order for her to learn more about politics. After an apparent Separatist attack, Thompson confronted his apprentice about her and Amidala's meeting with Onderon's senator Mina Bonteri on Raxus Secundus and how foolish it was. Despite that, Thompson's apprentice learned that politics were not as black and white as she had previously thought.[137]

Dathomirian threat

Thompson and Stone commanded Republic forces in a battle against Asajj Ventress's fleet in the Sullust system. Directly participating in the battle with their starfighters, Stone was singled out and attacked by Ventress, whose Ginivex-class fanblade starfighter was, in turn, disabled by Thompson. As Stone and Ventress crashed in the hangar of the Separatist command ship, Thompson soon landed to assist his former master; together, the two Guardians engage Ventress in a duel. They were interrupted, however, by the destruction of the ship, secretly ordered by Count Dooku. The two Guardians fled in their interceptors, presuming Ventress to be doomed.[139]

After the massacre on Devaron,[140] Thompson and Stone were sent to Dathomir, as footage had shown the Council of a Dathomirian's role during the attack on Devaron. Once on Dathomir, they made their way to a village, where they were ambushed by the Nightbrothers until Thompson took their leader hostage; the Zabrak then told the Guardians that a Nightsister had taken one of them to their fortress, prompting the Guardians to go there. Thompson and Stone questioned Mother Talzin, who told them his name—Savage Opress—and whereabouts. The two Guardians then headed to Toydaria where they confronted Opress, but failed to stop him from killing King Katuunko. Using the king's ship as theirs was destroyed, Thompson and Stone caught up with their target on Dooku's Separatist flagship; however, Opress managed to overpower them, forcing the two to retreat and leave the ship.[138]

Killing a defector

Following their encounter with Opress, Thompson and Stone were called away from a blockade at Herdessa to meet with Yularen. The admiral informed them that Mekedrix had gone missing. In addition, an execution squad known as the Death Wind had been striking at Republic and Separatist targets. The group's namesake, the Death Wind Corridor also passed by Mekedrix's home planet of Roon, creating a possible link. Yularen went on to say that the Death Wind may have taken up a covert position on Ando. As such, Thompson and Stone were to locate the Death Wind and kill Mekedrix if he was involved with them. Thompson initially refused, believing that killing a potential ally would make the Guardians like the Separatists. Stone assured Thompson that they would attempt to find a peaceful solution before they departed for Ando. After arriving on the planet and pushing through the local swamplands, the Guardians came upon empty sets of clone armor being used as effigies. Upon reaching the terminus of a river, they discovered an outpost built from the ruins of a Shadow Knights temple. They were then met by the Death Wind, who allowed the Guardians to see their leader in exchange for their lightsabers. Entering the outpost, Thompson and Stone came upon Mekedrix, seated on a throne and wielding a sword. Mekedrix claimed he had saved his victims by showing them the truth; that the purpose of existence was to kill and die. When Mekedrix revealed that his sword was made from the bones of a clone trooper, Stone asked him what the clone's name was. Stone went on to claim that life gave the galaxy meaning and that death had no use for a sunrise, a name, a home or a friend. Mekedrix then lunged at Stone, but Thompson retrieved his Lightblade and stabbed Mekedrix through the chest. Thompson expressed regret over killing him, but Mekedrix assured that this was the quickest way back to the sunrise before he passed away.[141]

Personal and wartime conflicts

The Destiny of the Chosen One

When the Guardians Council received a 2,000-year-old Guardians distress signal, Thompson, along with Stone and Tano, were sent by the High Council to the Chrelythiumn system, where they were supposed to meet up with Captain Rex and an armed cruiser. Though they both made it to the rendezvous point, neither Rex's nor the Guardians's ship could see each other. In fact, the Guardians's ship was guided to a mysterious planet, Mortis, where they met by a mysterious figure called the Daughter, who asked Thompson if he was "the one" and who wanted to lead them to the Father, when Thompson was separated from Stone and Tano, and the Daughter.[59]

Thompson tried to follow her, only to reach the monastery, where he met the Father and was given shelter for the night. As he tried to sleep, the figure of his mother, Shmi, appeared before him and encouraged him to tell her of his pain and guilt—that he was too late to save her, and that he had failed as a Guardians and that he had failed her, having tasted only vengeance when he slaughtered so many to avenge her death. But, as he mentioned his wife, who he claimed was "everything" to him, the figure finally revealed itself to be the Son, briefly assuming a monstrous appearance before disappearing.[59]

Shaken by the encounter, he confronted the Father, who told of his family's history, and wondered if he was truly the Chosen One. Though Thompson dismissed the prophecy as a myth, the Father arranged a test to see whether the Guardians Knight was fit to fulfill his destiny. Ordering his children to kill Tano and Stone, the Father goaded Thompson into choosing between his master and his apprentice, but Thompson instead controlled the two beings, bringing them to their knees. Having proved himself to be the Chosen One, the Father pleaded with him to stay, but Thompson refused, leaving with Tano and Stone on their shuttle.[59]

They were interrupted when the Son kidnapped Tano, prompting Thompson to pursue him, at the cost of damaging their ship near Son's cathedral. Despite Stone's reservations, Thompson went after his Padawan and climbed the tower to find Tano, corrupted by the dark side. Thompson fought her, soon to be joined by Stone, only to be interrupted by the Father's arrival. Stone tried to hand Thompson the Dagger of Mortis, but Tano grabbed it and gave it to the Son, who killed her, for she was no longer useful; the dark side user then mortally wounded his sister as she stepped in to save her Father. Thompson begged the Father to save Tano, and the dying Daughter used Thompson as a conduit to channel the last of her life Energy into Tano, resurrecting her. The Father told them to leave the planet before the Son used their ship to do so.[142]

The ship's repairs forced them to stay a bit longer, though, and Thompson went to the Father for guidance. On their meeting, the Father was determined to kill Son, but refused Thompson's offer of aid, telling the Guardians Knight to look inside himself for the answer for what was to be done. Leaving him, Thompson encountered The Energy spirit of his first mentor, Qui-Gon Jinn. Though Thompson sought his advice, Jinn advised him to remember his training and trust his instincts, and to go to the Well of the Dark Side to face his most personal challenge. There, the Son forced Thompson to confront his future, one consumed by the dark side. Horrified, Thompson submitted to the Son as he promised him the power to avert this destiny. Soon, Stone arrived, and Thompson left him trapped at the well, claiming to have seen the truth—that it was the Guardians who would stand in the way of peace.[31]

Thompson arrived as his Padawan disabled the ship and went to rescue Stone, while the Son went to steal the Dagger. As Thompson waited, the Father appeared before him and recognized that his son had broken the rules of time; he wiped Thompson's memory of these future visions, leaving him unconscious, and brought him to his monastery. Thompson awoke to find the Father, Stone, and Tano, moments before the Son arrived with the Dagger. To end the conflict, the Father impaled himself to distract his Son, while Thompson killed the Son with his Lightblade. As the Father also died, he warned Thompson to be wary of his feelings as they could lead to his downfall. As was noted by the Father,[31] Thompson had brought balance to The Energy[7] on Mortis through the deaths of The Energy wielders, and he still had a chance to do so for the full galaxy.[31]

After the Father's death, the three Guardians were transplanted back to the galaxy to finally rendezvous with Rex. Surprised to learn that, to Rex and the others, they had only been gone for a moment, they returned to the Worm Destroyer.[31] They would later report this encounter to the Council, with Thompson even mentioning that he had spoken with Jinn to Yorin. Even though Thompson was not entirely convinced, they came to believe it had merely been an illusion of the old mentor.[143] In reality, it had truly been the spirit of Jinn.[144]

The Citadel

More than one year and nine months into the war,[146] following the capture of Guardians Council member Even Piell by the Separatists, Thompson and Stone were briefed by Master Plo Koon and sent along with their team—which included Rex, Cody, Echo, and Fives—to infiltrate the Citadel on Lola Sayu to retrieve Master Piell, who held the information on the Nexus Route. Refusing to let Tano risk her life on such a dangerous mission, Thompson devised a strategy to bypass the lifeform scanners of the prison; the whole team underwent carbon-freezing and were transported to the Citadel by Commander Artoo and his reprogrammed battle droid squadron.[147]

After landing, Thompson was thawed out and he found, to his surprise, that Tano had disobeyed orders and followed him. Forced to take her along, the team made its way to the facilities, leaving the astromech to guard their shuttle. After free-climbing to the entry point through a cliff wall blasted by a strong gale and littered with electro-mines, and after Tano deactivated the ray-shield on the entrance, the team was able to enter, except for Charger, who fell to his death and alerted the prison staff, led by Osi Sobeck, of their presence. Inside, they lost Longshot, but finally made it to Master Piell, whom they freed and who told them they needed to rescue his officers.[147]

Despite the many traps laid through the fortress, the team rescued the officers, when they met the other carrier of the Nexus Route coordinates, Captain Wilhuff Tarkin. The team was split into two—while Stone's team created a diversion, Thompson would lead the others away. In Tarkin's company, Thompson and his team escaped through the cavern system below the prison, when Tarkin expressed some skepticism about the Guardians's plan, to which Thompson told Tarkin that he reserved his trust to those who understood gratitude.[147] However, they soon found common ground, for they both believed the Guardians Code prevented the Order from going far enough to achieve victory in the ongoing war,[148] and Tarkin developed a grudging appreciation for Thompson.[146]

As they made their way out of the fuel line to rendezvous with Artoo and the shuttle, they were attacked by droids and forced to join Stone at their pick-up point, with the shuttle heavily guarded. During the skirmish, Echo tried to defend the shuttle, but its destruction and subsequent explosion seemingly killed him. As they ran into the cave system, Thompson and Stone contacted the Council, telling them they needed to escape, and Koon immediately departed to pick them up.[148] They contacted the Council again to be informed of the extraction point; as they proceeded to the location, Sobeck sent anoobas after them. The creatures claimed Master Piell's life, who passed his intel on to Tano, and the group took a moment to honor his death. Ultimately, they got to the extraction point on time, and fled on Plo Koon's rescue shuttle, with Saesee Tiin's fleet covering them to allow their escape back to Coruscant.[145]

Padawan lost

Thompson, Tano, and 501st forces fought on Felucia alongside Plo Koon and his clones in taking out a reinforced Separatist outpost. Breaking their forces into three groups, Thompson and Rex's team were responsible for attacking the front gate and destroying the tactical droid, TZ-33, securing the outpost together with Koon and Tano's teams.[150]

However, Thompson then noticed that Tano had disappeared during the battle, and ordered a perimeter sweep. Thompson had his men search around the outpost, to no avail several times over. Master Koon then informed that he had alerted all forces throughout the Outer Rim and assured Thompson that she would be found eventually, but also firmly told him that their mission on Felucia was finished and that they had to return to Coruscant. However, Thompson refused to abandon her, to which Koon observed his emotions were clouding his judgment. Thompson countered "I will not leave her fate up to others." Koon insisted, "It's time to go." Only then did Thompson comply and call everybody in to leave the planet.[150]

Back at the Temple, Thompson relentlessly searched for possibilities in the Worm charts, which prompted Koon to suggest that he should trust in Tano's abilities.[150] Following the events on Wasskah, Thompson's search for his Padawan came to an end when she arrived to the Temple on the Halo, in company of her Wookiee allies and the younglings O-Mer and Jinx. Upon seeing her, Thompson profusely apologized for all that happened, heavily blaming himself for his apparent inability to protect her. Tano, however, assured him that his instruction gave her the skills necessary to survive and thanked him for his guidance. The two bowed to each other in newfound mutual respect under the delighted eye of Master Yorin.[149]

Battle of Horain

Sometime later Thompson participated in the Battle of Horain, alongside Stone and Rex. Thompson was contacted by Rex, who was requesting assistance, though he was already preoccupied with a group of Vulture Droids. Thompson later met Stone and Rex in disbelief, noticing they had hijacked an AAT alongside faulty battle droid Bats.[151]

Skirmish on Retta

On Retta, Thompson ambushed a group of B1 battle droids alongside Grand Master Yorin. Thompson was soon attacked by Kreeda, a Segredo who was attempting to scare the Guardians away. However, Kreeda soon became friendly to the Guardians, as she was an old friend of Yorin. When Thompson attempted to recruit Kreeda as a spy, the Segredo left, and Thompson and Yorin were attacked by battle droids soon after. After being separated with Yorin, he met the Guardians Master and Kreeda again, the latter of whom explained she was protecting refugees of the Clone Copulation. Using a hat from one of the individuals she was protecting, Thompson pretended to be using the "cloaking cap" to distract General Flebek, allowing Yorin to take out a B1. Believing there was no secret to invisibility and that it had been a trick, Flebek fled the area and boarded her ship. Upon asking what his report would entail, Yorin told Thompson that he would tell Mace Windu there was nothing of interest on Retta.[152]

Battle of Mon Cala

After the assassination of King Kolina of Mon Cala and new discord in the fragile peace between the Mon Calamari and the Quarren, Thompson and Amidala, sent as representatives of the Republic, arrived to the planet at the behest of Captain Gial Ackbar of the Mon Calamari Guard and protector of young Prince Lee-Char, but so did Riff Tamson, Separatist ambassador. Though Thompson and Amidala tried to mediate between the parties, the Quarren left the council hall, dissatisfied with the Mon Calamari rule. Along with Ackbar, they contacted Yorin and Windu, who dispatched a company of clone troopers equipped for underwater combat under the command of Kit Fisto and Tano.[154]

In the meantime, during the Quarren attack assisted by Separatist aqua droids, Thompson and Amidala accompanied Lee-Char, who in turn was bodyguarded by Ackbar. He engaged the Quarren soldiers until he was recalled by his wife to help Senator Meena Tills, while leaving Tano to cover the prince and rescue him from Tamson. As the first Separatist/Quarren assault came to an end, Thompson reunited with Amidala, Tano, Fisto, Ackbar, Lee-Char, and Tills to await the second assault, this time assisted by the Hydroid Medusas, which proved incredibly effective against the Republic-Mon Calamari army. Forced to retreat into the caves below, they regrouped to decide their next course of action—to retreat to the surface, only return to the sea floor as their frigate was destroyed and they were attacked by more enemies.[155]

They split into two groups: Thompson went with his wife, Ackbar, and Tills, whereas Fisto, Tano, and two troopers accompanied the Prince. Expecting Republic reinforcements to come soon, Thompson and his group proceeded to the Mon Calamari central planetary scanner facility, where the Guardians Knight collapsed the entire structure with The Energy, rendering planetary defenses blind to upcoming Republic reinforcements—the Gungan Grand Army, and Representative Binks, sent at the behest of the High Council. However, Tamson used Trident-class assault ship to create whirlpools, disorienting the Republic-Gungan forces, and though Thompson managed to destroy one of them, all but Tano and Lee-Char were captured.[155]

Brought before Riff Tamson, Thompson and Fisto were restrained by electric eels, whereas Amidala and Jar Jar Binks were imprisoned within containment devices, interrogating the two Guardians about the whereabouts of Prince Lee-Char. During the interrogation, Tamson attempted to Energy them to talk by biting a tiny hole into Amidala's helmet, allowing her suit to fill up with water. However, Tamson left to see the located Prince in person, Thompson and Fisto used The Energy to remove the water from her helmet, while Binks used his saliva to seal the puncture, saving her from drowning. The four were soon taken to witness Prince Lee-Char's execution, in which Nossor Ri, the Quarren leader, saved the prince at the critical moment and turned his people against the Separatists, resulting in the victory of the Mon Cala people. With the peace restored, Thompson and his friends were present for the coronation of Lee-Char, now recognized by both Mon Calamari and Quarren as their new monarch.[153]

Following rumors that the Gungans planned to aid the Separatists in an attack on Theed, Thompson accompanied Amidala to Theed and later, to the Gungan territory near Lake Paonga where they met with Binks, who confirmed the troubling rumors. He took the couple to Otoh Gunga, where they spoke to Boss Lyonie, and realized he was under the influence of a mind-controlling necklace, which Thompson telekinetically snatched, making him recover; Lyonie told them that new minister, Rish Loo, had given it to him.[156]

Thompson, Amidala, and Binks assisted Lyonie as he confronted Loo, only to face droid commandos, and for Lyonie to be stabbed by Loo before the minister's escape. Taking the Boss to an infirmary, the couple noticed the uncanny resemblance between the unconscious Lyonie and Binks, who they convince to impersonate Lyonie and cancel the Gungan assault on Theed. As he did so, uncovering Loo's deception to the army, Thompson pursued the fugitive minister to a trap set by Count Dooku in Loo's laboratory. As Dooku killed Loo and revealed his secret input in the Battle of Naboo, Thompson dueled the Count, only to be overwhelmed by Dooku's powers and his four MagnaGuards.[156]

Dooku then contacted Amidala and offered an exchange—the Guardians Knight for General Grievous, who had been captured by the Gungan Grand Army, thanks to Gungan General Tarpals's sacrifice. An hour later, Thompson was brought and handed over to Amidala, Queen Neeyutnee and the Gungans, while Grievous was allowed to remain free.[156]

Battle of Umbara

During the campaign on Umbara, Thompson and Stone discussed their attack strategy against the Umbaran capital city, which was being defended by Confederate and local militia forces. Together with Rex and Fives, Thompson prepared to repel enemy reinforcements striking against Stone's joint attack with Guardians Masters Saesee Tiin and Pong Krell, who would be supporting Stone from the south whereas Thompson and 501st Legion would advance from the north.[64]

Amidst heavy fire, Thompson and his battalion made it to the landing site, and then to a ridge which they used as a staging area. As he and Rex awaited the rest of their men, hoping to move out soon and reinforce General Stone's battalion, Dogma reported that all platoons had reported in, and Thompson told him to get some rest. Dogma replied that he was fine, but Rex told him it was a direct order, prompting Dogma to do as he was told. As Rex observed Dogma was "wound tight but loyal," Thompson joked that he reminded him of Rex, who disagreed, saying that was him in the old days.[64]

Shortly after this, the Umbarans ambushed Thompson and his troops, but an airstrike by Master Krell and Odd Ball saved them. After the quick save, Krell approached Thompson with a disconcerting message: the Council had ordered Thompson back to Coruscant, effective immediately, as requested by Supreme Chancellor Paladin, for reasons unknown. Though reluctant to leave his troops in the midst of battle, Thompson was forced to relinquish command over his troops to Master Krell and make the journey back to Coruscant.[64] Despite Krell's efforts to sabotage the Republic effort by tricking the 501st and the 212th battalions into attacking each other, Stone was successful in taking the capital and routing the remaining Umbaran forces, securing all sectors of the planet for the Republic.[157]

After ten rotations of Yorin's last contact with the Togruta colonists on Kiros, Thompson, Tano, Rex, Cody, Stone, and their forces arrived at the world to investigate its silence, with Thompson particularly worried they would be too late. After landing, suspicious of the lack of colonists in the town, they used BARC speeders to break through the city and surround the governor's tower, used as the Separatist Headquarters. The enemy commander, Darts D'Nar, then sent a message to Stone to discuss terms of surrender. Thompson then took the holoprojector out of Cody's hand and called D'Nar "Zygerrian scum." Though Thompson wanted to deal with the slaver himself, Stone told him to locate the missing colonists while he negotiated. As Thompson contacted Admiral Yularen to conduct a planetary bioscan to find them, Stone explained Thompson's behavior to Tano.[158]

Overhearing from Stone's negotiations that D'Nar had planted bombs throughout the city, Thompson and Tano set out to disarm them. Despite sniper droids, they destroyed the bombs in the nick of time, just for Stone to tell them D'Nar was escaping on the Tecora. With some effort, they managed to board the ship, inside which D'Nar pitted a blixus against them, but Thompson managed to throw it off the ship, while Tano bested D'Nar in the cockpit. Soon, Thompson joined her and, with frightening intensity, threatened the Zygerrian into revealing the colonists' location, much to Tano's surprise. However, it proved effective as D'Nar revealed that his Queen would hold the Royal Slave Auction.[158]

Zygerria

When Thompson, Stone, Yularen, and Tano told the Guardians Council of their findings, they were tasked with locating the colonists.[158] On the Tecora, and disguised as Zygerrian, they land on the capital city of Zygerria; splitting into two teams (Stone and Rex went to located the colonists), Thompson and Tano went to meet Queen Miraj Scintel, with Thompson posing as "Lars Quell" and bringing news of Bruno Denturri's supposed death, and presenting Tano as a present. He quickly won Scintel's favor, with the Queen inviting him to accompany her during the slave auction, where she intended to have Thompson prove himself a slaver by torturing Stone.[159]

Instead, he freed Stone and tried to escape with Artoo returning their lightsabers, Rex providing cover fire and Tano confronting the Queen. Despite their efforts, however, they were subdued, but Scintel saw Thompson's determination, for five electro-whips were needed to reduce him. Despite Dooku and half her kingdom demanding his death, Scintel had Thompson brought to her quarters. There he woke up and interrogated Scintel about his friends' whereabouts, while he throttled the Queen with The Energy. However, Scintel gasped that his friends would die unless he obeyed her, and Thompson was forced to comply and become her bodyguard and escort, just as she became infatuated by him.[159]

After a few days, Dooku would arrive, and Thompson observed that she too was a slave to the Count, before she left to receive the Shadow Knights Lord. With the help of Artoo, he escaped his guards and freed Tano from her cage and sent her to prep the ship, while he interrogated Scintel to tell him Stone and Rex's location. However, he found himself before Atai Molec, who had betrayed Scintel, and Dooku, with whom Thompson futilely dueled before Dooku blamed him of killing Scintel before her guards. Thompson then took Scintel and escaped to the Tecora, aboard which she told him Stone was in the Kadavo system and that she, just like Thompson, was a slave, before dying.[160]

After contacting Plo Koon to send reinforcements, Thompson and Tano soon reached Kadavo and entered the slave-processing facility. During the battle that ensued, Thompson disabled the turrets that were attacking Koon and his forces, while Tano saved the Togruta colonists from falling to their death (as caused by Keeper Agruss) by placing Admiral Barton Coburn's Arquitens-class light cruiser Hand of Justice underneath the facility. Along with Rex and Stone, Thompson evacuated the facility before it was destroyed on Koon's orders, before returning to the rest of Koon's fleet.[160]

Paladin kidnapped

After the capture of Moralo Eval, rumored to be the mastermind of a plot to kidnap Supreme Chancellor Paladin, Thompson, Tano, and Stone were recalled to an emergency meeting by the High Council while they walked through the nightly streets of Coruscant. On their way to the Temple, however, a sniper opened fire at them, which prompted the three Guardians to chase their aggressor. However, the sniper killed Stone before escaping on a speeder, prompting Thompson to return to his master, to find Tano crying and his old master dead, whom he called several times trying to get him to respond. Becoming withdrawn and grim, Thompson would not say a word since then, until after the funeral held to honor Stone, when he went on a self-imposed mission to locate his murderer, Rako Hardeen, at Trueping's. Though Thompson would rather kill him, he arrested Hardeen and ferried him to the Republic Detention Center.[122]

After hearing that Hardeen had escaped from jail, Thompson held a meeting with his friend, Paladin, when Windu informed the Chancellor that they would remove the bounty on Hardeen and the two other escapees, Eval and Cad Bane; a decision did not sit well with Thompson. Paladin suggested that the Council perhaps did not trust him to control his feelings, and Thompson felt the Council was doing nothing to catch the murderer of his master—his best friend. Exploiting this to his advantage, Paladin told him not to deny his feelings, for they were what made him special, and deliberately sent Thompson and Tano to Nal Hutta. There, two Guardians harshly interrogated locals for information, which led them to Orondia, the fugitives last known location.[162]

After spotting their quarry as they prepare to leave, Thompson rammed their ship into submission and board it in midflight, but was foiled by Bane and a subsequent crash-landing of both parties resulting from the chase. Surrounded by smoke, Hardeen left the ship, and Thompson confronted him, ready to kill Hardeen when Bane interrupted him. Hardeen intervened then and wrestled with Thompson, whispering to Thompson's ear "Anakin, don't follow me." As he felt a connection, Thompson passed out, only for Tano to protect him for Bane's attempts to execute him.[162] As they returned to Coruscant, Yorin summoned Thompson to his chambers and revealed the truth to him—that Stone was "Rako Hardeen."[161]

Afterwards, Thompson was briefed along his Padawan about the security plans the Council had for the Chancellor during the Festival of Light on Naboo. Thompson, Tano, and Windu would escort the Paladin and Mas Amedda to Theed, where Thompson was greeted by his wife. At the festival itself, the abduction was initially successful with Cad Bane and Moralo Eval ferrying Paladin away, while Thompson and Windu captured Embo and Twazzi; however, Stone stopped Eval and Bane, long enough for Windu and Thompson to arrive and take the two into custody.[163]

As he reunited with his old master, he voiced his resentment for the Council's lack of trust in him as well as Stone's decision to hide the truth from him. Wondering how many other lies he had been told by the Council, he set out on his own to bodyguard Paladin, who continued to place seeds of doubt in Thompson until they were greeted by Dooku. After destroying two of his MagnaGuards, Thompson dueled the Count with ferocity, until Stone arrived to help him rescue the Chancellor, whereas Dooku fled.[163]

A phantom menace

Sometime later, when Thompson and Tano were on their way to rendezvous with a cruiser, they decided to land at the Stobar Spaceport, as Thompson was hungry and tired of government rations. The two walked into Plop Dribble's, only to find police droids and an unconscious waitress. Though they attempted to investigate the assault, both the police and its owner, Loubo, told not to worry; instead, he offered them a free meal. However, Thompson sensed a disturbance in The Energy, something sinister and familiar—which, unbeknownst to him, heralded the return of the now renegade Shadow Knights Lord Maul.[164]

Onderon Rebellion

Following a plea made by a Freedom cell on Onderon, the Guardians Council considered how to help them fight the Separatists. Thompson suggested training the rebels in subversive combat tactics; though it verged on terrorism, which the Guardians would not support, the High Council decided to send advisors to the Freedom cell for the upcoming battle. On the Nu-class transport Valkyrie 2929, Thompson, Tano, Rex, and Stone reached the planet during the night, when they met Steela Gerrera and were guided to their Freedom secret base, where they met her brother, Saw. The team trained the rebels to combat droids, until Separatist probe droids discovered their outpost and attacked, which prompted the cell to enter Iziz.[166]

After some successful operations, Thompson, Stone, and Rex decided to return to Coruscant and report to the Council, while Tano stayed behind as a liaison to the rebels, without revealing herself as a Guardians.[167] They kept in frequent communication with her, to provide guidance,[167][165] but also to provide help by hiring Hondo Ohnaka to deliver missile launchers to the Onderon rebels for the final assault. Thompson attended the funeral service for Steela Gerrera.[168]

Losing Satine, saving R2

Thompson was later present when the Guardians High Council met up with the chancellor to discuss Maul's return, during which they decided to leave him alone even though they knew he was still alive.[169] Thompson lent the Twilight to Stone, who used it to travel to Mandalore to rescue Satine Kryze. With all its technical problems, after landing on Mandalore, Stone vowed not to borrow another ship from Thompson. However, as he escaped, the ship was shot down by Mandalorian commandos and destroyed, moments before Kryze was murdered by Maul during the Mandalorian civil war.[170] Thompson was waiting for Stone when the Guardians Master returned to the Temple hangar, where Thompson saw through Stone's attempt to hide his sorrow. Stone explained he had lost someone important to him and that he had struggled with his rage, something Thompson knew all too well. Thompson offered to come with him for a report to the Council, which Stone happily agreed to.[171]

Thompson was later present when the Guardians Council sent Meebur Gascon and a team of Republic droids to steal an encryption module from the Separatists.[172] Later Thompson participated in the Republic strategy conference on the space station Valor, while on the station he noticed the Renown was going a little too fast. Tarkin told Thompson that they are scanning Rhydonium on the ship, Thompson instantly realized it was a bomb, Thompson was later contacted and informed by Gascon of R2's sacrifice. However, Thompson refused to believe that his friend was gone. He sent out salvage teams to scour the wreckage, who retrieved R2's remains and repaired him. The two reunited, and Anakin was happy to have him back.[173]

Nearing the end

Tano's trial

During the defense of Cato Neimoidia, Thompson and Tano engaged Separatist forces on their Eta-2 Actis-class interceptors, until they received an urgent mission recalling them to the Guardians Temple. Its hangar had been bombed and, as the two had been off-world during the attack, Thompson and Tano were to lead the investigation to uncover the criminal—likely to be a Guardians. As they entered the Temple's hangar, they discussed the possibilities a Guardians was behind the attack. Tano doubted a Guardians could bomb such a sacred location to the Guardians,[175] and in the process, kill their own colleagues and employees.[176] However, Thompson told Tano to keep an open mind, and he reminded her there were Guardians that did not agree with their methods, and went rogue, such as Dooku and Krell. As a result, everyone who was at the Temple during the bombing were potential suspects, including Guardians.[175]

As Thompson and Tano walked across the burning hangar, they met Russo-ISC, whom Thompson sent alongside Tano to interview the witnesses, whereas he meditated at the hangar. Unable to discover anything, Thompson met up with Tano and Russo, who told him about their missing suspect, Jackar Bowmani. With this clue, Thompson went to speak with Chief of Security Cin Drallig. As Thompson approached Drallig on the Guardians Temple's steps, he witnessed a crowd of angry protestors, rallying against the Guardians for their violence during the war. After Thompson showed Drallig the hologram of Bowmani on an imagecaster, Letta Turmond identified Bowmani in the hologram and informed the Guardians she was Bowmani's wife. Later Thompson and Tano cornered Turmond near her home, and she under pressure admitted to feeding Bowmani nano-droids but would not reveal her motives.[175]

Thompson was present at Yorin's eulogy for the six Guardians who died during the explosion, and later took the opportunity to speak with Tarkin. Thompson and his Padawan were then summoned to the war room to be briefed about a Separatist attack on Saleucami, when Tarkin informed them that Turmond had requested to speak with Tano. When she was framed with Turmond's death and imprisoned, he was forbidden to see her, and only returned to track her down on her escape, following Tano into the pipelines.[177]

Alone, they revealed that their trust in each other was absolute, but as Thompson pleaded for her to surrender herself and make her case to the Council, she refused to take the blame for something she had not done and asked Thompson to trust her, just as she jumped onto a passing ship, thus escaping to the Coruscant underworld.[177]

As Tarkin declared she was being accused of sedition, Yorin assigned Thompson and Plo Koon to search for Tano, with some resistance from Windu—who did not believe Thompson to be emotionally detached to do what had to be done. Nevertheless, when she was captured, Thompson escorted her to the Temple,[178] where he was later summoned to the Chamber of Judgment along with his Padawan.
